([Evref-BL/claude-code-inactivity](https://github.com/Evref-BL/claude-code-inactivity/tree/HEAD/skills/review))
- **review**: Use when the rocket router selects review, or the user asks to review, audit, assess or check a PR, branch, diff, files, plan or design without asking for changes. ([Exxzzent/rocket](https://github.com/Exxzzent/rocket/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/review) · 2 copies)
- **review**: Review an assigned artifact against explicit correctness and verification criteria. ([eymenarabaci/piko](https://github.com/eymenarabaci/piko/tree/HEAD/skills/review))
- **review**: This skill should be used when the user wants feedback on Rust code they have written. Use when the user asks to "review", "check", "look at", "critique", or "give feedback on" their code, or after they finish implementing something and want to know if it's idiomatic, correct, or could be improved. This is a teaching review — not a rewrite. ([eywalker/rust-learning](https://github.com/eywalker/rust-learning/tree/HEAD/skills/review))
- **review**: Multi-agent code review for this repo — three independent Opus reviewers (idiomatic Rust, security/secrets/data leaks, general correctness) followed by auditor agents that verify every finding against the code, with results written to .claude/review/. Use when the user asks to "review", "run a review", "audit the code", "review my changes", "review the whole repo", or "review &lt;crate/file/feature&gt;". With no argument it reviews uncommitted changes; "all"/"repo" reviews the whole repository; anything else is treated as the named target (paths, crates, a feature, a commit range). ([f-squirrel/journal](https://github.com/f-squirrel/journal/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/review))
- **review**: Richiesta code review — dispatch subagent reviewer con contesto preciso. Feedback classificato Critical/Important/Minor. ([fabiognastri-tech/curiosa](https://github.com/fabiognastri-tech/curiosa/tree/HEAD/skills/review))
